資料中心監管疑慮衝擊 AI 供應鏈，美股全面重挫
2026 年 11 月 06 日
一、 市場動態
1. 資料中心監管疑慮引爆 AI 供應鏈賣壓，美股選後漲勢一日反轉：民主黨取得參、眾兩
院多數，美國進入分裂政府格局。S&P 500 於 11 月 4 日先漲 1.58%，11 月 5 日卻重
挫 4.36%，創 2025 年 4 月以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 跌 5.52%，費半大跌 12.25%，
十一大產業全數收黑。主因有三：一是資料中心開發成為州級政策議題，德州州長當
選人主張暫停新建，全美新增容量約三成可能受影響；二是高通與 Arm 財報不如預

期；三是建設遞延將推高依賴外部融資業者的持有成本。費半重挫前已自 6 月高點回
落近三成，政策不確定性進一步放大跌幅（詳見表一）。
表一：美股主要指數表現（2026/11/05 收盤）
主要指數 收盤點位 單日漲跌幅
S&P 500 指數 7,353.73 −4.36%
Nasdaq 綜合指數 24,315.00 −5.52%
費城半導體指數 9,011.14 −12.25%
資料來源：Reuters、Nasdaq、元大銀行整理
2. 波動率指數急升，公債殖利率全面走低，油價反彈：11 月 5 日避險情緒升溫，VIX 盤
中升至 28.09（前日 16.61）。資金湧入美債，2 年、5 年、10 年及 30 年期殖利率分
別降至 4.46%、4.59%、4.84%及 5.25%，呈牛市陡峭化；12 月升息一碼機率由約
83%降至約 65%。布蘭特原油反彈 3.88%至每桶 98.29 美元，通膨前景仍具不確定性
（詳見表二）。
表二：美債殖利率與避險資產變化（2026/11/05）

10 年期公債殖利率 4.84% −6 bps
VIX 波動率指數（盤中高點） 28.09 前日收盤 16.61
布蘭特原油期貨（美元／桶） 98.29 +3.88%
12 月升息一碼機率（CME） 約 65% 前日約 83%
資料來源：Bloomberg、WSJ／CME、元大銀行整理
二、 下跌原因
1. 民主黨橫掃國會及州長選舉，資料中心開發成為州級政策議題：德州州長當選人 Gina
Hinojosa 主張暫停新建資料中心，俄亥俄州與喬治亞州當選人也提出限制。地方政府

不另行通知。
可透過電價分攤、建設許可及稅務優惠影響新案進度，UBS 估計 2028 年全美新增資
料中心容量約三成可能受影響。國會對科技巨頭與資料中心用電的監督亦可能增強，
半導體、電力設備及資料中心類股成為賣壓重心。
2. 高通手機晶片業績低於預期，加重半導體類股賣壓：高通財年第四季調整後 EPS 年減
28%至 2.17 美元，手機晶片營收年減 26%至 51.37 億美元，低於預期，主因蘋果相
關收入減少；汽車與物聯網營收雖年增 36%，仍難抵銷缺口，股價隔日收跌 9.5%，
進一步放大半導體跌幅。
3. 融資成本與投資回收壓力加重，依賴外部融資業者首當其衝：資料中心資本支出多發

生於上線前，若電力、許可或工程延後，收入認列隨之推遲，利息與持有成本卻須持
續負擔。儘管 12 月升息機率降至約 65%，借款成本仍高，依賴外部融資或單一客戶
合約的 AI 基礎建設業者將面臨回報壓縮與再融資風險。
4. 科技股在本次重挫前已明顯修正，評價落至極低水準：費半自 6 月 22 日高點 14,634.72
點已回落逾 30%，Nasdaq 亦自 10 月 6 日高點 27,599.89 點回落逾 10%。市場質疑
AI 投資效益，降低對高評價企業的容忍度，政策不確定性因而放大修正。近期跌幅較
屬估值下調而非基本面惡化，若晶片訂單與獲利預估維持穩定，市場有望回溫。
表三：11/5 主要科技股及類股表現
標的 單日漲跌幅 重點說明
費城半導體指數 −12.25% 選後跌幅最深，AI 供應鏈評價重估
高通（Qualcomm） −9.5% 手機晶片營收年減 26%，低於預期
軟體類股 ETF（IGV） −4.48% 跌幅明顯小於半導體
資料來源：Reuters、Bloomberg、元大銀行整理

三、 市場看法
1. 短期評價修正而非長期趨勢反轉，AI 資本循環仍處擴張階段：我們視本波為多頭格局
中的健康修正。修正反映股東對龐大資本承諾要求更高風險補償，而非 AI 變現失速：
Google Cloud 第三季營收年增 103%、營業利益年增 209%，四大雲端業者資本支出
預估上修至約 1.16 兆美元，顯示企業看見更大市場而主動加碼（詳見表四）。此外，
政策衝擊集中於州與地方，聯邦仍支持資料中心擴建，選舉結果本質是要求用電成本
內部化，而非否定 AI 投資。
表四：AI 基本面持續擴張──關鍵指標一覽
指標 最新數據 解讀
Google Cloud 第三季營收 年增 103% AI 需求快速轉化為收入
Google Cloud 第三季營業利益 年增 209% 獲利能力未因擴張而惡化
四大雲端業者資本支出預估 約 8,732 億→1.16 兆美元 企業主動加碼，投資循環仍擴張
資料來源：元大投顧、Bloomberg；元大銀行整理

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。 
2. 留意五大反彈訊號，利空逐步消化後市場可望回穩：（一）州級電價與併網制度若於
2027 年上半年建立，延後專案可望下半年重新加速，眾議院以 417 比 3 通過
《Ratepayer Protection Act》亦顯示跨黨派共識；（ 二）雲端業者資本支出指引未下
修、未見取消訂單；（ 三）11 月 10 日 CPI 及 13 日 PPI 物價月增率回落；（ 四）財政
部維持附息債標售規模，資金回流市場；（ 五）VIX 回落且投資等級債利差穩定。訊號
出現後，市場有望回歸基本面。 
3. 政策先影響建設速度，AI 需求仍須以訂單驗證：州級限制首先改變的是資料中心選址
與交付時程，而非 AI 需求本身。雲端客戶若維持支出計畫，專案可調整順序或轉往他
州，惟設備交付遞延將壓抑供應商近季營收；聯邦立法限制則須經國會及總統否決權，
短期落地難度高。若主要客戶訂單穩定，本次修正較屬建設時程遞延的風險重定價。 
4. 新增算力面臨遞延，既有算力相對有利：高盛估計可能收緊開發限制的州約占美國規
劃中資料中心容量的 45%，其中德州約占 25%。新案放慢下，大型雲端業者既有算力
可望受惠於利用率與議價能力提升，資本支出延後亦減輕現金流壓力；仍待併網的專
案及高槓桿業者則面臨回收遞延，產業鏈分化將擴大。 
5. 歷史經驗顯示選後股市多正向，後續表現仍宜留意基本面變化：彭博統計 1950 年以
來，美國期中選舉後 12 個月 S&P 500 平均報酬約 15%，19 次全數收正，最高為 1954
年的逾 30%，最弱的 1986 年亦小幅上漲。府會分治抑制政策大幅轉向為選後行情主
要支撐，惟後續表現仍須觀察基本面(詳見下圖)。

6. 企業獲利仍強勁成長，本次修正以估值與風險溢價重估為主：彭博 11 月 4 日統計，
S&P 500 已公布財報公司中 79.85%優於預期，資訊科技更達 92.45%，第三季 EPS
成長率分別為 29.0%及 67.8%（詳見表五）。 財報為美股提供中期支撐，短期評價則
取決於 2027 年獲利預估能否承受建設遞延與融資成本壓力。 
表五：S&P 500 第三季財報統計（截至 2026/11/04） 
類別 已公布家數 優於預期家數 優於預期比率 第三季 EPS 成長率 
S&P 500 397 317 79.85% 29.0%

資料來源：Bloomberg；元大銀行整理
7. 最新經濟數據仍具韌性，聯準會利率路徑仍具變數：初領失業救濟金降至 18.9 萬人，
第三季勞動生產力季增年率 3.6%、單位勞動成本季減 0.7%，經濟仍具韌性。惟油價
逼近每桶 100 美元，若通膨高於預期，升息定價恐再升溫，壓抑科技股估值與長債價
格。
8. 信用市場是判斷風險是否由股權評價擴散至金融條件的重要指標：若 AI 企業持續擴大
資本支出與外部融資，償債能力將受關注。大型投資等級科技企業現金流穩定，較能

吸收壓力；高槓桿或依賴單一客戶的 AI 基礎建設業者則較易受利差上升衝擊。信用利
差若維持穩定，風險仍以股權評價調整為主；若同步擴大，則須警惕金融條件收緊。
四、 投資建議：
1. 短期修正不改 AI 長期趨勢，切勿恐慌殺低錯殺優質標的：本次重挫源自選後政策不確
定性與評價重新定價，AI 需求與企業獲利尚未反轉，屬多頭中的短期波動。應避免恐
慌出清而錯殺優質標的；已持有者以續抱為主，加碼宜分批逢低，避免高槓桿操作。
2. 以股債平衡穿越短期波動，等待基本面重新主導市場：我們認為，本次修正屬短期政
策衝擊與評價調整，中長期科技股展望仍正面。在政策與通膨明朗前，建議將集中度
過高的科技股部位，適度轉向波動較低的平衡型或多重資產商品，搭配優質債券穩定
收益，並保留股票部位分批布局優質標的，待反彈訊號確認後再提高風險資產比重。
3. 股票部位聚焦優質、評價回落且前景較佳的產業：修正後部分優質企業評價已回到合
理區間，股票建議聚焦：一、握有長期電力合約、已通電園區的大型雲端業者；二、
明年展望佳的 AI 供應鏈，如 ASIC、光通訊、電力設備、半導體設備及資安；三、跌

幅較小的軟體及非科技優質股。高度仰賴新建資料中心與外部融資的業者，宜待環境
明朗再評估。
4. 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價
調整期間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現
階段不建議延長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利
率敏感度與資產配置穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中
及再融資需求可控的發行人仍較為穩健，對 AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合
約現金流與債務到期結構。

公司簡介

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

義隆 (2458 TT) 
受累終端需求疲軟

永豐觀點 
終端應用以筆電為重，受終端需求不確定性較大。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持中立：由於整體 PC 市況疲軟，又義隆電本身模組產品也受記憶體與被動元件
短缺影響，加以製造成本攀升，使得公司營運承壓，研究部下修獲利預估值。研究
部預估義隆電 2026 年稅後 EPS 6.07 元(原預估 9.03 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 5.54
元(原預估 9.81 元)。義隆電因產品線集中筆電領域，市況仍顯弱勢，又製造成本上
升使毛利率承壓，維持中立建議。

ESG 評析 
義隆企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。

義隆電為筆電觸控板與觸控螢幕晶片大廠：義隆電為全球筆電相關晶片大廠，其中
公司於觸控板市佔率>50%、觸控螢幕晶片市占率>50%、Pointstick 為市場主導
者，筆電指紋辨識市占率 30%~35%。義隆電產品結構 82%觸控相關，18%來自
MCU、Pointstick 與關係企業，公司產品線主要競爭對手為 Synaptics。義隆電晶
圓代工廠夥伴為聯電、台積電與 Magnachip。  
3Q26 獲利欠佳：義隆電 3Q26 營收 22.25 億元(-30.7%QoQ，-33%YoY)，毛利
率 44.7%，營業利益率 13.3%，稅後獲利 1.42 億元(-84%QoQ，-82%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 0.47 元，獲利欠佳。義隆電 3Q26 各項財務細項如下：(1) 營收下滑顯著，
主要是客戶提前拉貨、消費電子需求疲軟、長短料不匹配而影響對義隆電晶片拉貨
(CPU 與記憶體為短料)。義隆電 3Q26 僅指紋辨識持平，其餘產品均下滑(AI 產品
線/觸控板滑落幅度較小)，觸控螢幕/指向裝置下滑幅度較大，公司 3Q26 產品結
構為觸控比重 73%(觸控板 43%/觸控螢幕 14%/指紋辨識 16%)、非觸控比重
13%(指向裝置 9%/MCU 與其他 4%)與 AI 產品線 14%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的
47.4%降至 44.7%，主要是(製造成本攀升)、產品結構轉差(高毛利率的觸控螢幕與
指紋辨識下滑較多)與被動元件/PCB 漲價，由於公司多項產品屬於模組形式，故毛
利率亦受此影響。(3) 業外損失 1 億元(前季認列業外收益 2.94 億元)，主要來自金
融資產評價損失。 
4Q26 營收仍有挑戰：由於 3Q26 業外損亦超逾稅前獲利 10%，故義隆電本次法
說未給予展望，而由 10/2026 營收僅 4.61 億元(-23%MoM，-54%YoY)，反映市
場整體需求仍顯弱勢。研究部預估義隆電 4Q26 營收 15.76 億元(-29%QoQ，-
45%YoY)，毛利率 44.9%，稅後獲利 1.17 億元(-18%QoQ，-84%YoY)，稅後 EPS 
0.39 元。 
受筆電市況疲軟衝擊：研究部認為義隆電主要業務受累於筆電市場疲軟，使得營運
表現受波及，公司預期 2027 年以車載、無人機更有表現力道。(1) 車載導入電動
物流車與智慧交通。義隆電表示今年電動巴士成長翻倍，第六個客戶將於 2027 年
步入小量量產，而若政府通過電動物流車補助措施，則預計 2027 年亦將導入電動
物流車供應鏈，體量將高於電動巴士。(2) 義隆電無人機產品為相機模組(包括固定
焦距、變焦與夜視功能等)、AI box 與避障偵測，今年導入雷虎等客戶系統，明年
將參與歐美日展覽，期待營收進一步擴增。針對光通訊產品維持預計 EIC2H27 送
樣。義隆電藉由入股 PETA Optronics 15%~16%，而導入光通訊研發，義隆電將
與 PETA 共同設計開發電晶片(包括 TIA 阻抗放大器、雷射驅動晶片等)，處理高頻

高速的電子電路與電訊號傳輸，預計 2H27 送樣。針對 Synaptics 被 On-Semi 收
購影響，隨 Synaptics 產品線轉移，逐步降低行動裝置的領域，義隆電在商務筆電
市占率已得到提升。 
維持中立建議：由於整體 PC 市況疲軟，又義隆電本身模組產品也受記憶體與被動
元件短缺影響，加以製造成本攀升，使得公司營運承壓，研究部下修獲利預估值。
研究部預估義隆電 2026 年營收 102.27 億元(-17%YoY)，毛利率 46.5%，稅後獲
利 18.44 億元(-25.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.07 元(原預估 9.03 元)，並預估 2027 年
營收 90.42 億元(-11%YoY)，毛利率 46%，稅後獲利 16.84 億元(-8.7%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 5.54 元(原預估 9.81 元)。義隆電因產品線集中筆電領域，市況仍顯弱勢，
又製造成本上升使毛利率承壓，維持中立建議。

### Page 1

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
Growth Intact Despite A Longer HVDC Transition
CITI'S TAKE 
We walked away from AES’s 3Q briefing more confident in the BBU growth 
outlook. Resilient low-voltage demand and higher-power product 
upgrades should sustain growth through the HVDC transition, with 
400V/800V commercialization targeted for mid/end-2027E respectively. 
Rising technical complexity should favor AES’s engineering expertise and 
established customer relationships. We trim our TP to NT$1,550 (from 
NT$1,680) on 25x 2027E P/E from 27x reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty. 
Reiterate Buy as rising BBU penetration and content per rack underpin 
structural growth.
3Q26: strong BBU demand drives growth and mix upgrades lift GPM y/y — 3Q 
sales of NT$5.2bn rose 27% y/y and 9% q/q, driven by strong BBU demand and were 
9% above our forecast. GPM expanded 1.3ppt y/y to 36.3% on mix upgrades, though 
it fell 2.8ppt q/q. Op profits rose 34% y/y with OPM up 1.4ppt y/y to 24.6%. Net profit 
of NT$1.1bn was up 34% y/y, 3% above our forecast but 5% below consensus. Higher 
opex and lower non-op income partly offset the sales beat.
Low voltage BBUs retain a substantial growth runway — Mgmt expects steady 4Q 
growth and remains positive on 2027E, with no downward revisions to customer 
demand plans. Continued datacenter buildout should sustain low-voltage BBU 
procurement while HVDC architectures undergo qualifications. Demand for 3kW 
products remains broad across conventional servers and lower power datacenter 
workloads. AES is working with several new CSPs and overseas SIs, with new 
customer contributions expected in 2027E. Rising GPU power, greater BBU 
penetration and higher content per MW should support volumes and mix-led ASP 
growth. Cell prices are rising, but AES indicates stable procurement through long-
standing relationships with suppliers and will seek to pass on cost increases. 
HVDC progress: commercialization likely in 2H27E — High voltage BBU 
commercialization has been delayed to 2H27E, reflecting product development and 
system integration challenges. Currently samples have been delivered and large-
scale testing is underway for 400V products, with mass production targeted for 
mid-2027E and 800V products are scheduled for end-2027E. Mgmt. sees high 
voltage BBU ASP to be 5-10x higher than low voltage products. We believe 
increasing system complexity and qualification requirements favor AES’s 
established engineering capabilities and CSP relationships, though shipment 
timing and margin contribution require monitoring.

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Non-BBU development: new applications broaden the growth base — Mgmt
expects non-BBU growth to outpace BBU growth in 2027E as new projects scale.
LEV demand should gradually recover following the inventory correction. Telecom
application has entered mass production in 2026, with mgmt. expecting the
business to double in 2027E. AGV, AMR, forklift and robotics projects involve
leading international customers, with AGV/AMR products expected to enter mass

production in 2H27E. These projects should diversify revenue and extend AES’s
battery pack expertise into additional markets.
Earnings revisions and TP change —We lift our earnings forecasts by 6% for
2026E mainly to reflect stronger sales strength and GPM in 2026E while we keep
our 2027/2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged. We slightly lower our TP to
NT$1,550 based on 25x 2027E P/E vs. 27x prior reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty.

### Page 5

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Advanced Energy Solution
Company description
Established in January 2020 and listed in March 2021, Advanced Energy
Solution Holdings (AES) is a holding company registered in Cayman Island,
supplying battery packs for LEV (light electrical vehicle such as e-Bikes) and
BBU (battery back-up unit for datacenters) applications. Prior to the
establishment and IPO, AES was a BU focusing on non-IT applications within

its parent company, Simplo Technology (6121.TW).
Investment strategy
We rate AES shares as Buy. Being an industry leader in battery pack design
and manufacturing with a proven track record, we expect AES to benefit from
higher BBU content per rack and deep CSP engagement as the industry
transitions from conventional 48V systems to HVDC architecture. BBU now
contributes >70% of sales and should remain the key earnings driver as AI
rack power continues to rise. We forecast earnings CAGR of 27% in 2025-

2028E, supported by strong BBU demand, mix upgrade toward high-power
BBU products and op leverage.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$1,550 is based on 25x 2027E EPS on par with its
historical average since listing and supported by a 2025-28E earnings CAGR
of 27%. We use 2027E as the year of valuation base as we think 2027E can
better reflect its earnings power after the commercializarion of HVDC
products.
Risks
Key downside risks to our earnings forecasts that could prevent the shares

from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected end demand
from e-Bikes post the pandemic and volaitlity in cloud capex deployment that
could impact datacenter BBU sales; 2) component supply
constraints/logistics challenges, which could lead to slower-than-expected
shipment and top-line momentum; and 3) peer competition, which could
impact sales growth and margins negatively, in our view.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
October  Sales +14% MoM/
+30% YoY
In this report, we focus on Delta's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• October sales came in at NT$74,666mn (+14% MoM/+30% YoY), driven by
the increasing shipment of power supply units, HVDC power racks, and liquid
cooling products.
• QTD revenue reached 35% of MSe at NT$215,881mn (+10% QoQ/+34% YoY),
and 34% of consensus at NT$219,806mn (+12% QoQ/+36% YoY).

Our view:
• We think 4Q26 revenue is tracking slightly ahead of market expectations.
• We think the slightly better Oct monthly sales should help on near-term
sentiment, though more meaningful upside to market expectations on
earnings growth might  depend on the penetration of HVDC power racks into
the coming years.
• We continue to see Delta as a key beneficiary of the increasing power
consumption of AI clusters through its comprehensive power/cooling

offerings. Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28e EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 59% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 170: 261112_gs_inventec

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_gs_inventec.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Neutral rating on Inventec with a new 12m TP of NT$56 (from NT$61), 
based on 14.2x 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E for the past 
10 years, and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 192: 7-9月期ユーロ圏GDP 減速しつつも上振れ

Source PDF: all report pdf/7-9月期ユーロ圏GDP　減速しつつも上振れ.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

欧州

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和

2026 年 11 月 2 日 全 6 頁

7-9 月期ユーロ圏 GDP 減速しつつも上振れ 
実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.4％と市場予想を上回る

ロンドンリサーチセンター シニアエコノミスト 橋本 政彦

[要約] 
 ユーロ圏の 2026 年 7-9 月期の実質 GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期
（同＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏の成長率の半分は、多国籍大企業の影響を受けたアイルランドの高成長によ
るものである点は割り引いて見る必要がある。ただし、アイルランドを除いてもプラ
ス成長を維持しており、エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の加速や、記録的猛暑
による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移したという評価
に変わりはない。 
 国別の内訳では、スペインが内需の堅調さを主因として前期比＋0.6％と高成長を維持
し、ユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図が続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、前期から
成長率は鈍化したものの、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回った。他方、フランスは同▲
0.1％と 2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じる低調な結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏経済は 10-12 月期も緩やかな景気拡大が続く見通しである。エネルギー価格
の上昇によるインフレ率の加速が個人消費を下振れさせるリスクには注意が必要だが、
ドイツを中心とした拡張財政がユーロ圏経済を下支えすると見込まれる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 6

成長の半分はアイルランドの上振れ、ただしそれを割り引いても底堅い 
ユーロ圏の 2026 年 7-9 月期の実質 GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期（同
＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査、以下同：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
ただし、7-9 月期の上振れには、毎期の振れが非常に大きいアイルランドの高成長が影響し
ている点は割り引いて見る必要がある。アイルランドの成長率は前期比＋5.5％と、成長率が
公表された 12 ヵ国の中で突出して高く、これがユーロ圏全体の成長のおよそ半分を占めてい
る。CSO（アイルランド中央統計局）は、多国籍企業の影響が大きいセクターが押し上げ要因
になったと報告しており、一部の多国籍大企業の企業内取引などが成長率を押し上げたとみら
れる。アイルランドを除くユーロ圏 20 ヵ国の GDP 成長率（大和総研試算）は同＋0.2％となり、
実態としての景気回復ペースはヘッドラインよりも緩やかだったとみるべきだろう。もっとも、
アイルランドを除いてもプラス成長を維持しており、エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の
加速や、記録的猛暑による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移した
という評価に変わりはない。 
国ごとの成長率を確認すると、スペイン、オランダ、エストニアがいずれも前期比＋0.6％
となり、既述のアイルランドの次に成長率が高かった。スペインの成長率は前期（同＋0.7％）
から減速したが、ユーロ圏全体に対する寄与度はアイルランドに次いで大きく、スペインの高
成長がユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図は 7-9 月期も続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、こち
らも前期（同＋0.3％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回り、ユ
ーロ圏全体の成長を下支えした。他方、不調だったのがフランスであり、増加を見込んでいた
市場予想（同＋0.1％）に反して、同▲0.1％と 2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じている。

（注）左図の「その他」は、ユーロ圏全体から表示された5 ヵ国（ドイツ、フランス、イタリア、スペイン、ア
イルランド）を差し引いて計算。

### Page 3

1

3 / 6

フランスは輸出・投資が減少、スペインは引き続き内需主導の成長維持 
ユーロ圏全体の 7-9 月期の需要項目別の内訳は速報段階で明らかになっていないため、以下
では内訳が明らかになっているフランスとスペインの需要項目別の動きを見ていく。 
フランスの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比▲0.1％と、2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。内・
外需別では、内需の前期比寄与度は＋0.5％pt とプラスだった一方、外需寄与度が同▲0.6％pt
となり GDP を押し下げた。内需の内訳では、在庫変動が最大の押し上げ要因（前期比寄与度：
＋0.4％pt）となった。ただし、内需から在庫を除いた国内最終需要も前期からわずかに増加
ペースが加速しており、GDP 全体としてはマイナス成長ながら、内需については一定の底堅さ
が見られたと評価できる。 
需要項目別の詳細を見ていくと、個人消費は前期比＋0.4％と前期から加速し、これが内需
を押し上げた。増加幅は 2024 年 7-9 月期以来の大きさであり、個人消費に限れば 7-9 月期はむ
しろ好調だったと評価できる。内訳では、財（同＋0.4％）、サービス（同＋0.4％）がいずれ
も前期から加速しており、個人消費は幅広く増加している。財消費では、エネルギーが同＋
1.2％と増加に転じたことが主な押し上げ要因となった。ガソリン価格が上昇する中、石油精
製品の減少は続いたものの、電力消費の増加がエネルギー全体を押し上げた。また、エネルギ
ー以外の財では、電気自動車の販売増加により、輸送用機器が同＋3.1％と好調だった。サー
ビス消費では、娯楽関連を中心とした家庭向けサービスの増加（同＋0.9％）、および宿泊・飲
食サービス（同＋0.7％）が主な押し上げ要因となった。 
好調だった個人消費とは対照的に、総固定資本形成は前期比▲0.6％と 3 四半期連続で減少
し、内需の足を引っ張った。最大の押し下げ要因となったのは、輸送用機器が同▲7.0％と大
幅に減少したことである。統計公表元の INSEE（フランス国立統計経済研究所）の報告によれ
ば、前期にあったクルーズ船の受け渡しの反動、および企業による投資の減少が押し下げ要因
となった。このほか、建設投資が同▲0.5％と 3 四半期連続で減少したほか、不動産取引の停滞
により、法律サービスなどのビジネスサービス（同▲1.6％ ）、 不動産サービス（同▲0.7％）
が前期から減少した。他方、IT 機器の増加により資本財が同＋0.8％と増加したほか、サービ
ス投資の中でも情報通信サービスは同＋2.1％と増加した。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比▲1.7％と 2 四半期ぶりに減少した。前期に大幅に増加した航空
関連が反動で同▲9.1％と大きく減少したことが減少の主因となった。また、農産品（同▲
7.0％）、農作物加工品（同▲1.1％）が減少したほか、資本財が同▲1.1％と 7 四半期ぶりの減
少に転じるなど、財輸出が総じて振るわなかった。サービス輸出は同＋0.5％と増加を維持し
たが、増加幅は前期（同＋1.0％）から減速している。 
輸入は前期比＋0.1％と、小幅ながら増加した。自動車、および海軍装備品の増加によって
輸送用機器が同＋0.3％増加したことや、資本財の増加（同＋1.4％）が押し上げに寄与した。
他方、石油精製品（同▲6.4％ ）、 農作物加工品（同▲1.6％）が減少したことで、輸入全体の
増加幅は小幅にとどまった。

### Page 4

1

4 / 6

図表2 フランス・スペインの実質GDP 成長率と需要項目別寄与度

（出所）INSEE、INE、Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

スペインの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.6％となり、4-6 月期（同＋0.7％）から成長ペース
が減速しつつも、高い成長率を維持した。内・外需別では、内需の前期比寄与度が＋0.5％pt、
外需が同＋0.1％pt となり、引き続き堅調な内需が成長のドライバーとなっている。 
個人消費は前期比＋0.6％となった。形態別の内訳では、サービスが同＋0.9％と、15 四半期
連続で増加し好調さを維持したことに加え、耐久財が同＋0.5％と 2 四半期連続で増加し、非耐
久財は同＋0.6％と 4 四半期ぶりの増加に転じている。他方、半耐久財は同▲2.2％と減少に転
じた。 
総固定資本形成は前期比＋0.7％と、11 四半期連続の増加を維持した。ただし、増加幅は
2025 年 4-6 月期以来の小ささとなり、増加ペースは鈍化している。建設投資が同＋1.0％と堅
調さを維持する一方で、輸送用機械の減少が下押し要因となり、機械・設備・武器システムは
同＋0.2％と小幅な増加にとどまった。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比＋1.3％と 4 四半期連続で増加し、増加幅が前期から拡大した。
インバウンド（非居住者による支出）の増加（同＋1.6％）を中心に、サービス輸出が同＋
1.5％と増加し、輸出全体をけん引する状況が続いているが、サービス輸出の伸びは前期から
縮小した。他方、財輸出は同＋1.1％と、増加幅はサービス輸出に比べて小さいものの、前期
から増加ペースが加速している。 
輸入は前期比＋1.1％となった。サービス輸入が同＋2.1％と大きく増加したことに加え、財
輸入も同＋0.8％と増加した。輸入の増加幅は前期から縮小し、輸出の増加幅を下回ったため、
外需（純輸出）の寄与度は 2 四半期ぶりのプラスに転じている。 
なお、ドイツ（前期比＋0.2％）については、まだ内訳の詳細な計数は公表されていないが、

### Page 5

1

5 / 6

ドイツ連邦統計局からは、消費支出の増加が押し上げに寄与したこと、一方で総固定資本形成
が減少したことが報告されている。また、輸出については好調だった 2026 年上期から減速した
とコメントされた。イタリア（同横ばい）については、内需寄与度のプラスと、外需寄与度の
マイナスがイタリア国家統計局によって報告されている。これらの情報を総合すると、7-9 月
期は、個人消費を中心とした内需がユーロ圏の成長に寄与した可能性が高い。

防衛・インフラ投資が景気拡大を下支え、リスクはエネルギー価格 
ユーロ圏経済の 10-12 月期に入ってからの景気動向に関して、欧州委員会が公表するユーロ
圏景況感指数（総合）を確認すると、2026 年 10 月は前月差▲0.6pt と 2 ヵ月連続で低下した。
もっとも、過去の関係に照らせば 10 月の指数の水準は、景気悪化を示すほどの低さではなく、
ユーロ圏経済は引き続き緩やかなペースでの拡大が続いていることを示唆する。 
景況感指数の内訳を見ると、消費者信頼感指数が前月差▲1.1pt と 2 ヵ月連続で低下したほ
か、これに平仄を合わせる形で小売業が同▲1.5pt と低下した。これは、エネルギー価格の上
昇によってインフレ率のさらなる加速に対する懸念が高まっていることや、生活不安を理由と
した各地での抗議活動の広がりが影響したとみられる。実際、10 月に入って大規模なデモや暴
動が発生したフランスの消費者信頼感指数は、同▲3.4pt と主要国の中でも目立って大きく前
月から悪化した。サービス業の景況感は依然として高水準ながら、同▲0.6pt と 6 ヵ月ぶりの
低下に転じたのも、消費者マインドの悪化や、それを背景とした需要の減少が下押し要因とな
った可能性が高い。

図表3 ユーロ圏の景況感指数と実質GDP 成長率（左）、景況感指数の内訳（右）

（出所）Eurostat、欧州委員会より大和総研作成

一方、家計部門の弱含みとは対照的に、鉱工業の景況感はこのところ堅調さを増している。
鉱工業の景況感指数は、前月差＋0.5pt と 5 ヵ月連続で改善し、2023 年 4 月以来の高さとなっ
た。鉱工業の景況感の回復には、拡張財政による防衛支出・インフラ投資関連の需要の増加が
50

6 / 6

寄与しているとみられる。足元では長期金利が上昇ペースを速めており、これが設備投資や公
共投資の拡大を抑制する恐れはあるものの、積極的に財政を拡張させるドイツを中心とした防
衛支出、インフラ投資がユーロ圏経済を下支えする構図は当面続くと見込まれる。 
先行きのリスク要因としては、引き続きエネルギー価格の動向、およびその影響の広がりが
焦点となる。今回の 7-9 月期の GDP 統計では、インフレ率が加速する中でも個人消費が底堅く
推移したことが確認された。だが、原油やガスなどのエネルギー価格は上昇が続いており、実
質所得や消費者マインドへの下押し圧力は、今後さらに強まる可能性がある。とりわけ冬季の
需要期にはエネルギー価格上昇による負担感が一層強まるとみられ、個人消費の下振れに警戒
する必要があるだろう。

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 11. 2  
방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang@lssec.co.kr

## Document 256: _美元已死_？不，美元强势归来：DXY突破关键位，空头再遭重创

Source PDF: all report pdf/_美元已死_？不，美元强势归来：DXY突破关键位，空头再遭重创.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美元指数突破关键技术位，升⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平。美国经济表现、利率优势及外
资流⼊共同⽀撑美元，投机资⾦也持续加码多头。但过去⼀年创纪录的 1.47 万亿美元美国
资产投资，也可能成为美元的潜在⻛险：⼀旦美国资产吸引⼒下降或外汇对冲需求上升，
美元或⾯临回调压⼒。
摘要：
美元多头重新掌握主动权，看空美元的投资者再次落空。美元指数（ DXY ）近期突破关键技术
位，升⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平。美国经济数据相对其他主要经济体表现较好，加之利率差异
仍对美元有利，为美元提供了⽀撑。
从技术⾯看，美元指数重新站上 200 周均线，与 2021 年底的⾛势呈现相似形态，且尚未出现明显
超买信号。与此同时，投机资⾦正在回流。据三菱⽇联⾦融集团（ MUFG ）数据，杠杆基⾦美元
多头头⼨已升⾄ 8 ⽉初以来最⾼⽔平，市场看涨情绪有所升温。
美元⾛强的另⼀⾯是欧元持续承压。欧元兑美元已跌⾄ 200 ⽇均线下⽅，初步下⾏⽬标指向
1.10 。不过，美元当前的重要⽀撑因素之⼀ —— 外资对美国资产创纪录的 1.47 万亿美元投资，也

可能成为未来最⼤的⻛险来源。⼀旦美国资产相对表现逆转，资本流⼊放缓或对冲需求上升，
都可能改变美元⾛势。
DXY 突破关键技术位，多头信号全⾯点亮
美元指数近期持续⾛强，最新突破将其推⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来的最⾼⽔平。从技术指标看， DXY 已
重新站上 200 周均线， 200 ⽇均线则持续向上倾斜。相对强弱指数（ RSI ）尚未进⼊超买区域，
上⽅ 105 附近的主要趋势线构成下⼀道重要阻⼒，在此之前，美元仍有⼀定上⾏空间。
更⻓周期来看，⾃ 2011 年以来的美元主要上升趋势线再次提供⽀撑。据 LSEG Workspace 数
据，当前技术形态与 2021 年底⾼度相似。当时美元在类似的技术结构下开启了⼀轮显著升值⾏
情。
技术指标尚未发出明显的反转信号，也为美元多头继续押注提供了依据。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

经济表现差异与利率优势提供⽀撑
美元⾛强并⾮单纯由技术⾯推动，美国与其他主要经济体之间的增⻓表现差异，以及利率差
异，同样是重要⽀撑。
花旗经济意外指数显⽰，美国经济数据整体好于市场预期，但这更多反映的是相对于市场预期
的表现，并不意味着美国经济不存在增⻓压⼒。与此同时， MUFG 数据显⽰，美元⾛势与相对收
益率保持较⾼相关性。只要美国相对于其他经济体的利率优势没有明显收窄，美元就仍将获得
⽀撑。
欧元区的情况则相对不利。欧元区与美国之间的经济意外指数差距⼤幅收窄，反映出欧洲经济
数据相对表现偏弱，欧元兑美元也随之⾛低。
更令欧元承压的是，多项潜在利好均未能转化为汇率⽀撑。能源价格⼤幅下跌、欧洲央⾏释放
偏鹰派政策信号，都未能有效提振欧元，法国财政⻛险则进⼀步加重市场担忧。在相对经济表
现和利率差异均不利的情况下，欧元仍⾯临下⾏压⼒。
外资创纪录流⼊美国资产，为美元提供强⼒托底
除了经济和利率因素，外国资本持续流⼊美国资产，也构成美元的重要⽀撑。
据 MUFG 数据，截⾄今年 8 ⽉的⼀年内，外国投资者购买美国股票和企业债券的规模达到创纪录

的 1.47 万亿美元，接近全球⾦融危机前历史峰值的两倍。
由于外汇对冲⽐例仍处于低位，这些资⾦流⼊转化为直接的美元需求，进⼀步⽀撑美元⾛势。
美国资产的相对吸引⼒不仅影响美股表现，也对美元形成⽀撑。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
40 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
图⽚
投机性持仓同样印证了这⼀趋势。 MUFG 最新持仓数据显⽰，杠杆基⾦的美元多头主要集中在做
多美元兑欧元和加元，其中欧元空头头⼨已升⾄ 2020 年 3 ⽉以来最极端⽔平之⼀。
这意味着，美元⾛强已不只是基本⾯因素推动的结果，投机资⾦也在进⼀步强化这⼀趋势。
1.47 万亿美元资本流⼊：最⼤⽀撑或成最⼤⻛险
不过，当前⽀撑美元的最强劲⼒量，未来也可能成为其最⼤的⻛险来源。
MUFG 警告，如果美国股市相对其他市场的表现持续逆转，外国投资者对美国资产的需求可能随
之降温，进⽽削弱美元的资本流⼊⽀撑。
值得注意的是，美元⾛弱并不⼀定需要外国投资者⼤规模抛售美国资产。即使他们继续持有美
股和企业债券，只要开始提⾼美元对冲⽐例，也可能显著改变外汇市场的供需关系，削弱此前

由资本流⼊带来的美元需求。
因此，下⼀轮美元趋势逆转的导⽕索，可能⾸先出现在美国股市，⽽⾮外汇市场本⾝。 MUFG 认
为，美国股市相对表现和外国投资者的持仓变化，将是判断美元强势能否持续的关键指标。
短期来看，技术⾯、利差和资⾦流向仍共同⽀持美元多头。但如果美国资产的相对吸引⼒开始
减弱，当前⽀撑美元的庞⼤资本流⼊，也可能逐渐转化为美元上涨周期的隐患。

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 điểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 điểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có khả năng dòng chảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi lựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đầu tư công, chính sách tiền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn).

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 329: FX Outlook 2027_ Seven key calls

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Outlook 2027_ Seven key calls.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We expect 2027 to be a low-volatility year in FX, with central
banks and rate diﬀerentials ﬁrmly in the driving seat. We see a
dollar decline resuming from 2Q27, while diverging monetary,
growth and external dynamics create opportunities across
regions

Chris Turner, Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Deepali
Bhargava
Winners and losers in FX next year

### Page 2

Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
Not a breakout year for volatility
Our most important FX call for 2027 is the market environment. Our
macro-driven call is that lower oil prices and only a modest
recalibration in global monetary policy mean that FX volatility can
stay low. This is not 2022/2023, when major central banks
belatedly delivered 500bp tightening cycles to deal with double-
digit inﬂation. If 2026 has shown us anything, it is that FX markets
can prove quite resilient in this high-growth, high-inﬂation global

economy. And markets have proved quite resilient to geopolitical
risk as well.
For the majority of the last ﬁve years, the market has
overestimated FX volatility. There may be some structural factors at
play here, such as huge growth in ﬁnancial assets bringing in more
liquidity to the FX market and the greater use of FX pricing algos on
the buy-side, limiting the market fallout when trading conditions
thin.
Yet market volatility has not vanished. There are plenty of risks out

there, which we highlight in a dedicated section below. The same
applies for market liquidity. If the shock is big enough – such as
Liberation Day tariﬀs in 2025 or the start of the Iran war this year –
market depth can fall away quickly. The prospect of fat tails, low-
probability, high-impact event risks, is certainly a threat to global
FX markets. But the bar for these risks to raise volatility is a high
one.
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.

Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 3

Volatility has mostly undershot expectations
in 2026
Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
Look no further than central banks in
G10
In a year dominated by geopolitical risk and energy shock, currency
investors quickly moved beyond headline trading and increasingly
looked to short-term rate diﬀerentials as the primary guide for
currencies. We expect that to remain the case next year.
Central bank tightening is moving beyond its “insurance” phase,
requiring evidence that inﬂationary pressures are becoming

persistent. Oil prices will remain part of the story, but we think the
more important distinction is between economies where the shock
can generate more lasting inﬂation and those where it cannot. On
top of that, the central banks’ FX-speciﬁc approach will be key.
Remember how, in 2022-2024, the fear of ampliﬁed inﬂation
eﬀects from a weak currency prompted tighter ﬁscal policies
among some often dovish-leaning central banks.
Here are our top picks among G10 currencies for next year:

1. JPY – the best risk/reward. We don’t have a huge
outperformance of the yen in our forecasts, especially relative
to the euro. But that’s primarily due to our assumption that the
euro will drop its French-related risk premium by end-2028, and

### Page 4

a carry-favourable environment can somewhat contain yen
gains. However, we think the yen oﬀers the best risk-reward
proﬁle in G10. Alongside being sharply undervalued based on
long-term fundamentals, it faces limited risks of dovish
repricing as the Bank of Japan’s tightening cycle predated the
Iran war. It incidentally has strong safe-haven value against
some fat-tail risks (AI bust, ﬁscal dominance, dollar
debasement, some geopolitical events) and stands to be a

beneﬁciary of an ultimate capitulation in USD rates. Downside
risks are, crucially, limited by a coordinated Tokyo-Washington
eﬀort to prevent sharp USD/JPY rallies.
2. CHF – SNB turning pro-franc. If 2027 is the year when global
interest rates peak and then reverse, the opportunity cost of
holding the franc starts to fall. We also think the Swiss National
Bank will prove more tolerant of franc strength. Not only are
there fewer complaints about the strong franc from local

corporates, but Switzerland’s inﬂation is much lower than
trading partners and the SNB might again opt for nominal
franc appreciation to keep the real exchange rate stable.
3. NZD – The high-beta gamble. Among higher-beta currencies,
we see good potential for the New Zealand dollar next year. The
Reserve Bank of New Zealand has delivered a few dovish
surprises, but we think it is underestimating the inﬂation risks
for 2027: we expect at least two hikes, also helped by a fairly

robust growth backdrop. NZD should be favoured against the
Australian dollar to play a decline in global rates, as the
Reserve Bank of Australia may already shift to cuts in the third
quarter of 2027.
Sterling, dollar and AUD face largest dovish
repricing risk

### Page 5

Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
Dollar decline is delayed, not cancelled
Our baseline for global inﬂation in 2027 remains benign, leaving
most of our major central bank calls more dovish than current
market pricing. We expect the Federal Reserve to deliver one ﬁnal
hike in December 2026, taking rates to a 4.25% peak, but we don’t
think the additional hikes priced by 3Q27 will materialise. Modest
second-round eﬀects should fail to sustain inﬂation once the 2Q27

base-eﬀect decline comes through. Importantly, and unlike in other
economies, we think benign inﬂation rather than weaker growth
will be the main catalyst for a dovish Fed turn. That means markets
may be reluctant to price a large Fed cutting cycle too early, but it’s
equally an environment where we envision a Fed cut in 4Q27.
Ultimately, we expect a nearly 100bp drop in the 2-year USD swap
rate by the end of 2027, with most of the move concentrated in the

year’s second half.
We expect the dollar to weaken alongside that repricing, with
greater conviction in the downside story later in the year. The Fed
should remain cautious about turning dovish before clearer
evidence of disinﬂation emerges, while elevated funding rates will
continue to make it expensive to sell or hedge dollars until some
easing is priced in. And because we also hold dovish views on other
central banks, we don’t expect narrower USD short-term rate

diﬀerentials to trigger a particularly large dollar downturn. We
target a roughly 5% decline in DXY to 97 by end-2027.

### Page 6

Front-end rates to drive DXY lower
Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
Bond premium on the euro needs bad
news fuel
Our colleagues in the macro and rates team remain cautious about
a recovery in French sentiment in the next couple of quarters. Weak
growth (0.5% in 2027) should continue to make ﬁscal arithmetic
complicated, and we forecast a deﬁcit of 5.5% next year –
assuming a budget is passed – above the 5.0% government target.
Election frontrunner Marine Le Pen has signalled large spending

cuts, although their credibility remains in question. She is, anyway,
a more market-friendly candidate than left-wing rival Jean-Luc
Mélenchon.
We think markets will be wary of jumping into a meaningful OAT
relief rally before the presidential election (18 April-2 May) and likely
parliamentary elections in the summer are out of the way. We are
not forecasting another big selloﬀ in French bonds but think 125-
150bp will be the norm for the OAT-Bund 10yr spread into 2Q27,

before some tightening towards 100bp in 2H27.
The euro has been embedding a French risk premium since early
October, meaning a further material deterioration in sentiment
would probably be needed to generate additional idiosyncratic
pressure. Historically, the link between euro-area sovereign spreads

and FX has been patchy, with stress episodes often requiring a

We therefore expect French political risk to keep euro sentiment
somewhat subdued into the elections and a subsequent bond-
market relief rally to oﬀer some support, but not to displace central
bank expectations as the main driver of EUR crosses next year. In
other words, we really need to see a signiﬁcant deterioration in the
French situation, prompting ECB bond market intervention, for

EUR already embeds French risk premium
Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
Central Europe: Same shocks, different
paths for local FX
The Central Europe region is entering a year of divergence. The
Czech Republic, Poland and Hungary face similar risks from energy
prices, geopolitics and volatile global markets, but increasingly
diﬀerent monetary, ﬁscal and growth dynamics mean CEE3 is
becoming less of a single FX trade.

In the Czech Republic, the Czech National Bank has already
delivered two hikes, taking rates to 4%, and remains the most
hawkish central bank in the region. If inﬂation proves more
persistent, further tightening remains possible. Positive nominal
and real rate diﬀerentials and high credibility should therefore keep
the koruna relatively defensive during periods of global uncertainty,

### Page 8

while any easing of external risks could put it back on an
appreciation path.
Poland is moving diﬀerently. The National Bank of Poland is still
waiting to deliver its ﬁrst hike, leaving the zloty with less monetary
support for now. Strong growth and sizeable EU inﬂows remain
important anchors, but narrower rate diﬀerentials, loose ﬁscal
policy and rising political and rating risks have weakened PLN’s
relative appeal. We therefore see the zloty as more vulnerable to

higher global yields, energy prices and geopolitical risks.
Hungary is heading in the opposite direction, with the National
Bank of Hungary having cut rates in recent months. We expect
another cut only later next year, provided global conditions allow it.
Attractive carry, EU-fund inﬂows and an improving external balance
should support the forint, while progress towards euro adoption
provides another positive longer-term story. The central bank’s

focus on the exchange rate should meanwhile limit the pace of
easing and keep EUR/HUF biased towards the stronger end of its
range.

Asia: Rotation underway, tech currencies
should lead

### Page 9

A softer dollar should provide a broad tailwind for Asian FX,
particularly in the second half of 2027, but we expect performance
to remain highly diﬀerentiated. We favour the Korean won and
Taiwanese dollar, expect further renminbi appreciation – albeit with
greater two-way volatility – and see continued downside risks for
the Indonesian rupiah and Philippine peso. We are more
constructive on the Indian rupee.
Current account strength should continue to favour the tech

currencies. Although the AI cycle may be plateauing, activity
remains high and strong export revenues should support the KRW
and TWD. Exporter FX conversion and measures encouraging the
repatriation of overseas assets should provide additional support.
CNY should also beneﬁt from strong external balances, although
we expect a less one-directional appreciation trend.
Rate support will become less favourable. Most Asian central banks
are further advanced in their tightening cycles than the Fed and

should ﬁnish hiking by early 2027 before preparing for easing.
Narrowing rate support should particularly weigh on the rate-
sensitive IDR. Continued household and corporate demand for
foreign assets could also oﬀset some of the beneﬁts from strong
current accounts.
Lower oil and food prices should help importers in the second half
of 2027, but capital ﬂows are unlikely to recover evenly. Political
uncertainty and the MSCI downgrade should also remain

headwinds for the IDR, while weaker FDI inﬂows leave the PHP
vulnerable. By contrast, we favour the INR, where resilient growth,
supply-chain diversiﬁcation and continued FDI should provide
support, while leaving India less exposed to swings in the AI cycle.

local currency (2Q26 vs 2Q25)

### Page 10

Source: ING, Macrobond
Latam: Banxico’s neglect of the peso
stands out
We’ve said that central bank policy is going to be more important
than ever in 2027. And exchange rates can be an important part of
the monetary toolkit – especially in small, open economies. In a
world where central bank tightening cycles are being priced more
hawkishly, Banxico’s unabashedly dovish stance stands out.
When cutting rates to 6.25% in November, Banxico noted that,

because of slack economic conditions, the pass-through of
exchange rate ﬂuctuations to consumer prices would be limited.
The IMF puts that economic ‘slack’ – or the negative output gap – at
0.4% of GDP in 2027. That makes Banxico far less concerned about
a weaker currency than most other central banks.
In addition, Banxico has now cut the peso’s policy rate protection to
just 225bp over the Fed. That is a record low and leaves the peso
vulnerable to any deterioration in the external environment. We see

the peso as a relative underperformer in 2027.
Further south, the Brazilian real has been causing a lot of pain to
investors recently. The fear is that President Luiz Inácio Lula da
Silva’s pre-election giveaways will come home to roost next year.
His choice of Finance Minister is going to be important, as will any
progress on a 2027 budget. Preventing a bigger sell-oﬀ in the real,
however, is the independent central bank running very high real

## Document 357: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-14).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
New Zealand 2026 General Election:
Preliminary results and next steps.
9 Nov 2026 |  Westpac Economics Team | westpac.co.nz/economics | economics@westpac.co.nz
Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
that might be requested. Parliament is required to meet
no later than 14 January (six weeks after the return of the
writ), providing an opportunity for a new government
to test that it has the confidence of the House. It is
worth noting that Parliament can be summoned and
begin functioning even if coalition negotiations are still
occurring. If no coalition or confidence-and-supply
arrangement has been reached by then, negotiations can

simply continue and the incumbent Government remains
in office under the caretaker convention (Ministers in
the outgoing government retain executive authority
temporarily, even if they are no longer MPs).
Given that the Pre-Election Economic and Fiscal
Update was released in late September there is no legal
requirement for the Treasury to publish a Half Year
Economic and Fiscal Update (HYEFU) this year. And given
that it will be some weeks before a government is formed,

it seems unlikely that there will be a government in place
that would request that a HYEFU be produced despite
there being no statutory requirement (certainly there
would be little time to incorporate policy changes that
fall out of new coalition agreements, thus diminishing
its value). Once a government is formed, it will have an
opportunity to lay out its immediate priorities in the
Budget Policy Statement (BPS), which by law needs to
be released before the end of March next year. The new

government could decide to release an interim fiscal
update with the BPS if that was thought necessary.
Potential coalition outcomes.
We see three main likely outcomes:
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First.
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First with the support of the Opportunity Party
on confidence and supply.
• Some coalition of Labour, the Greens, Opportunity and
Te Pāti Māori, potentially with Opportunity and Te Pāti

Māori sitting outside the cabinet.
Our preview of the election discussed the policies of
the main political parties, while the figure on page 5
highlights some points of commonality with the centre-
right and centre-left blocs.
National, ACT and NZ First share a broadly pro-
development and deregulatory agenda, including support
for greater resource development, fewer regulatory
barriers and compulsory or expanded KiwiSaver. Beyond
that common core, National and ACT align particularly

closely on lower taxes and deregulation, while National
and NZ First share an emphasis on infrastructure, regional
development and law and order. ACT and NZ First favour
tighter immigration settings and tighter welfare policies.
The differences are clearest at the edges: ACT advocates
deeper tax cuts, smaller government and more market-
led provision, while NZ First places greater emphasis
on economic sovereignty, regional development and
targeted support for working New Zealanders. National

occupies more of the middle ground, combining fiscal
discipline and a pro-business agenda with increased
infrastructure and housing supply.
The Opportunity Party presents itself as a middle
alternative that can work with the left or the right
groupings. They have five bottom line policies they say
they need to support any new government. These include
competition reform through enhanced Commerce
Commission powers, more restrictions on political
lobbyists and donors, a ten-year plan of investment in the

healthcare sector, an end to bottom trawling in inshore
areas and a phase out elsewhere and for the Government
to recommit to New Zealand’s Paris Agreement
obligations. Their success in meeting these bottom
lines with a new government and their flexibility around
these requirements will be key to their ability to form an
agreement with either side. There is better congruence
of the Opportunity Party’s policies with the left than the
right – so progress here will be critical to watch should

their support be required to form a new government. We
suspect they will be very motivated to avoid the need for
a new election via a hung parliament should push come
to shove.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Labour, the Greens and Opportunity share support
for increased public and social investment, a broader
tax base, greater infrastructure investment, stronger
competition policy, cheaper public transport and a
more active role for government. Labour and the Greens
overlap particularly on higher taxation, public investment
and stronger climate and environmental policy, while
Labour and Opportunity share an emphasis on social

investment and higher public-service wages. The Greens
and Opportunity both favour broader taxation and
stronger climate action, although their preferred tax
instruments differ. Their distinctive priorities are also
clear: Labour places more weight on worker protections
and restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate; the Greens on
environmental protection, progressive taxation and Te
Tiriti; and Opportunity on land-value taxation, housing
infrastructure, productivity, innovation and evidence-

based policy.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (October 28)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its 28 October meeting, 
extending its pause for a fourth 
consecutive decision as it balances 
inflationary pressures against uneven 
and subdued economic growth. Our view 
is aligned with fixed market income 
pricing. Headline inflation rose anew to 
2.8% yoy in September 2026, driven by 
higher energy, food, and core price 
increases, but remained within the upper 
end of the central bank’s 1-3% target 
range. As the BOT is still likely to look 
through what it views as temporary, 
supply-driven inflationary pressures, it 
will likely refrain from tightening policy, 
and keep interest rates accommodative 
to support the soft and uneven growth 
cycle.

European Central Bank (October 29)

We expect the ECB to extend its pause, 
keeping the deposit facility rate at 2.5%. 
Market participants will instead focus on 
the central bank’s assessment of rising 
French and Italian bond yields and 
widening spreads versus Bunds. While 
we do not expect direct references to 
individual countries, the ECB is likely to 
signal that recent developments in 
France reflect the country’s weaker 
fiscal and debt profile rather than a 
broader systemic concern. Policymakers 
will seek to differentiate between a 
localised bond sell-off and a Eurozone-
wide market disruption, justifying their 
decision to monitor developments rather 
than implement specific measures at this 
juncture. Meanwhile, inflation continues 
to rise, with little relief expected in 
upcoming readings given elevated 
global fuel prices. 
Bank of Japan (October 30)

The Bank of Japan is expected to keep 
the overnight call rate unchanged at 1.25% 
at this meeting, pausing after the 25bp 
hike in September. The BOJ is likely to 
assess the impact of the previous rate 
hike before taking further action. The 
recent stabilisation of the yen also 
reduces the urgency of another hike at 
this meeting.

The tightening bias should remain intact. 
Growth and inflation remain broadly on 
track, leaving room for the BOJ to revise 
up its FY2026–FY2027 GDP and CPI 
forecasts. Wage growth has also 
remained strong, with Rengo reportedly 
targeting wage increases of 6% in 2027, 
reinforcing the BOJ’s confidence that 
underlying inflation can be sustained 
around its 2% target. We continue to 
expect the policy rate to rise to 1.50% in 
December 2026 or January 2027, 
followed by a further increase to 1.75% 
by 2Q 2027.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Export growth is expected to accelerate 
from 53.0% yoy in August to 55.0% in 
September, in line with the pickup in 
China’s export growth from 25.0% to 
36.3%. As a key re-export hub for China,

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Hong Kong should benefit from robust 
AI-related electronics trade, with 
electrical machinery, equipment and 
parts exports to Hong Kong accounting 
for 20.6% of China’s total exports. 
Import growth is also expected to remain 
firm, supported by resilient export 
orders and gradually improving 
investment sentiment, sustaining 
demand for intermediate and capital 
goods.

Singapore

Singapore’s industrial production (IP) 
likely remained in expansion for a 13th 
consecutive month in September 2026, 
although we expect growth to moderate 
to 7.6% yoy from August’s strong 15.4% 
yoy pace. IP growth continued to be 
underpinned by the electronics and 
precision engineering clusters, which 
has been key drivers of Singapore’s 
manufacturing strength, reflecting 
sustained demand for artificial 
intelligence-related hardware. However, 
performance across clusters remained 
uneven, with chemicals and biomedical 
manufacturing likely contracting, and 
tempering overall gains.

South Korea

The preliminary estimate for 3Q26 GDP 
is likely to show growth remaining solid 
at 3.2% yoy, slightly below 3.7% in 2Q 
and 3.8% in 1Q. Nominal GDP growth is 
likely to remain in double digits, 
significantly outpacing real GDP growth, 
thanks to improved terms of trade amid 
rising memory export prices. By 
expenditure, exports picked up in 3Q in 
both nominal and real terms, supported 
by stronger semiconductor shipments. 
Investment also appeared robust, as 
reflected in the equipment investment 
estimation index and machinery order 
index. Private consumption, however, 
appeared to weaken amid the sharp 
KOSPI correction since July following 
the introduction of leveraged ETF 
regulations. Our full-year GDP growth 
forecasts of 3.5% for 2026 and 2.4% for 
2027 remain on track.

Taiwan

The preliminary estimate for 3Q GDP is 
likely to show growth remaining strong 
at 12–13% yoy, close to 12.9% in 2Q, 
albeit below the peak of 15.4% in 1Q. 
Exports maintained strong double-digit 
growth in 3Q (44.6% yoy in nominal 
terms and 21.1% in real terms), supported 
by robust shipments of semiconductors, 
servers and other AI-related products. 
Machinery investment also sustained 
strong double-digit growth, underpinned 
by higher capacity utilisation in the ICT 
sector and a resulting increase in capital 
goods imports. Private consumption 
appeared to have strengthened further, 
supported by stock market gains, 
improving wage growth, and the 
associated wealth and income effects. 
Construction investment remained the 
weak spot, weighed down by elevated 
inventories in the new-home market and 
the ongoing need for inventory digestion. 
We maintain our 2026 and 2027 GDP 
growth forecasts at 11.6% and 5.6%, 
respectively, with risks tilted to the 
upside.

• China’s exports growth should ease on slowing momentum 
• India’s CPI could bump up on higher food costs 
• Taiwan’s exports could moderate as the AI cycle normalizes

### Page 2

The Week Ahead November 6, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Taiwan

Export growth is expected to ease to 
44.4% yoy in October from 60.9% in 
September. A higher comparison base 
makes exceptional export growth 
difficult to sustain from 4Q onwards. The 
global AI cycle is also expected to settle 
into a more normal pace into 2027, as 
higher interest rates lift capital costs 
and US mid-term election results 
heighten regulatory uncertainty. These 
factors underpin our forecast for 
Taiwan’s GDP growth to return to high 
single digits from 4Q onwards and into 
2027.

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s industrial 
production growth to rise to 5.5% yoy in 
September 2026 from 5.0% yoy in 
August. The uptick was likely driven by 
export-oriented manufacturing as 
strong artificial intelligence (AI)-related 
demand continued to support growth in 
electrical and electronics goods, 
alongside a smaller contraction in mining.

China

Export growth is expected to decelerate 
from 36.3% yoy in September to 28.1% in 
October. Both the official manufacturing 
PMI and new export orders sub-index fell 
below 50 after two months in expansion 
territory. High-frequency trade indicators 
also point to slowing momentum, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage handled 
at China’s 20 major ports falling from 7.2% 
yoy in September to -4.2% in October. 
Freight activity weakened as well, with 
growth in international cargo flights 
dropping from 3.6% yoy to -5.9% over the 
same period. On the price front, factory-gate 
and raw material price pressures moderated, 
with the main raw material purchasing price 
and producer price sub-indices declining 
from 60.8 and 54.0 to 56.8 and 51.7, 
respectively. Despite remaining above the 
50 threshold, the moderation suggests some 
easing in pipeline price pressures. 
Downstream consumer price pressures are 
also likely to remain subdued amid soft 
domestic demand. As a result, CPI inflation 
is expected to ease slightly from 0.9% yoy in 
September to 0.8% in October.

India

We expect headline inflation to rise to 6.0% 
yoy in October from 5.5% the month before, 
on higher food costs and a low base effect. 
High frequency data pointed to a further 
pick-up in sequential pressures in food 
segments, across cereals, edible oil, 
perishables (vegetables) etc. Non-food 
components should find relief from further 
moderation in precious metals and delay in 
passage of retail fuel price changes. Core 
inflation is nonetheless set to quicken to 4.5-
4.6% as services notch increases and as 
indirect tax effects in the base, fall out of the 
equation. With inflation expected to test 
past the higher end of the RBI target range, 
we expect a second successive rate hike in 
December. The decline in oil prices has also 
led to cooling of overnight OIS rates as 
concerns over aggressive rate hikes have 
eased. Goods trade deficit is likely to stay 
wide at above $30bn in Oct.

類股的波動。但就趨勢來說，既有政策不會因
此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重
要支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間
輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢
熱門議題/資產影響推估 市場焦點
➢ 美國國會變天：民主黨不僅重新奪回眾議院，也
以遠超預期的幅度拿下參議院多數席次。整體政
策方向其實不會發生重大改變，但民主黨未來可
能利用財政法案與債務上限談判，推動限制對伊
朗戰爭、部分撤銷關稅以及影響財政支出。
➢ 市場焦點放在資料中心建置進程：AI資料中心的
建設是否受到影響，是目前市場最關注的焦點。
本行認為短期逆風確實存在，但不影響長期建置
趨勢。

美國國會變天，AI概念受挫然未見風險情緒，殖利率回落
AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊
盤勢回顧
美10年期利率回到5%以下，信用利差自高點收斂
3
• AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊：民主黨全面掌控國會後，市場擔憂AI產業、大型雲端服務與資料中心的監
管與審查將顯著收緊，使相關類股、日台韓股市均下挫，不過資金輪動到其他產業，支撐美股指數維持穩定。
• 財政紛擾仍存但支出展望中性，美10年債利率來到5%以下：市場開始反應財政政策可望趨於中性，再加上地
緣政治有望緩和，布蘭特原油跌破100美元/桶，支持美國公債利率持續走低至5%以下，自10月高點以來下滑
近50個基點。殖利率下滑輔以金融市場不確定因素降低支撐股市反彈，同步帶動美國信用債利差收斂，11月以
來美國信用債總報酬緩步走揚。
資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/11/5，右，Bloomberg，2026/11/5。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/5

©  CTBC
兩黨對立將放大財政期限影響與紛擾，整體財政支出趨於中性
兩黨對立，財政期限的重要性上升
政策影響
2027年的財政動能預計將大致維持中性
4
• 財政期限的重要性上升，帶來政治紛擾：民主黨可利用具強制性的時間節點，作為政策讓步的槓桿。第一個要
注意的時間點是12/11政府支出授權到期，不過將期限延到2027年2至3月的機率較高(否則政府可能關門)。另
外推論1Q27將臨近債務上限，以目前的財政部存款和非常規措施的使用，最多可支應至2027年8月至10月。
• 財政政策整體趨於中性：分裂政府通常意味著較不具擴張性的財政政策，而眾議院掌握聯邦預算的撥款權，將
可能透過拒絕追加或直接限制戰爭預算的方式來迫使戰事降溫。然民主黨同時也可能與共和黨協議，以撤銷去
年大而美法案的支出削減措施、恢復健康保險補貼等作為交換條件，讓白宮得以增加國防預算。綜合來看，短
線面臨預算協調的紛擾，美債殖利率波動仍可能偏高，但趨勢上政策制衡效果增強，有利減輕殖利率的壓力。
資料來源：左， 高盛，2026/11，右，高盛，2026/11。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11

©  CTBC
關稅結構可能改變，資料中心監管程度提高但不影響建置趨勢
民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化有限
政策影響
對資料中心監管程度將提高，但非禁止建置
5
• 民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化可能有限：民主黨領袖已表示將嘗試撤銷已實施的部分關稅，但
川普政府仍可利用301條款推動新的關稅，整體有效關稅稅率變化可能有限，但其中可能的重點是降低對加拿大
的關稅。
• 對資料中心監管程度將提高，不影響建置趨勢：傳統共和黨票倉的德州變天，而德州是最大的資料中心建設地
區，新任的民主黨州長可能要求企業自行承擔電力及電網擴建成本、保障居民用水優先權，並在保護規範明確
之前影響資料中心的建設進度。不過本行認為，除了要求企業揭露資料來源與訓練資訊、建立安全治理架構，
以及落實其他風險管理措施等規定之外，尚無太多州級監管制度被認為具有高度限制性。而在聯邦層級，大規
模的新監管措施即使被國會提出，也很可能被川普否決，且AI競爭力仍是跨黨派國策，建設趨勢將持續推進。
資料來源：左， 高盛 ，2026/10，右，高盛，2026/10。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

### Page 6

©  CTBC
AI 資料中心已從「產業政策」轉變為「民生電價議題」
超大型業者算力供給轉吃緊
產業影響
2030年電力缺口收斂
6
• 德州資料中心暫停令，AI算力布局的轉變與供需影響：德州8月初頒布的暫停令，同步凍結了新資料中心的興建
（僅限未動工新案）與電力輸送。在扣除被淘汰的虛報專案需求後，預估部分專案將外溢至其他電力更吃緊的州，
導致全美電力缺口淨擴大約 3–6 GW。若Hinojosa 就任後延續暫停令執行超過一年，其影響2028 年全美電力缺
口將進一步擴大，2030年供需缺口預估將逐步收斂。另一方面，暫停令讓2028年本就吃緊的算力市場更緊，維
持算力租金的高檔區間，對超大型業者而言建置成本增加，與手上既有的算力可以提高售價，兩者相抵，2028
年企業獲利的預估影響僅在1-3%水準。
• 提高監管是正在發生的實質逆風，喊停是低機率的極端政治口號：AI 技術攸關美國的核心基本國力。民主黨的
政策向來主張「調整與監管」，而非全面「廢止」。因此，未來更嚴格的許可審查與補助限制，勢必會提高雲

端大廠在德州擴張的時間成本與資本風險。然而，也可能推動針對資料中心的特定稅種，例如聯邦總收入稅，
或針對資料中心電力消耗的消費稅。民主黨也有可能在新會期提出比共和黨更強制性更嚴格監管的版本。民主
黨掌控國會後，最可能成法的是「電費成本分攤」立法，因為川普已將此議題列為自己的政績。
資料來源：左，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11，右，blackrock，2026/4。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/4

©  CTBC
健護最具兩黨協商的價值，醫療產業相對受惠
12/11為醫療補貼可以放進談判的時間點
產業影響
健護未來一年的本益比仍合理
7
• ACA補貼及醫療補助是最可能與川普達成交易的領域：民主黨贏得參眾兩院，不等於民主黨執政，兩黨仍須協
商。就健護產業而言，相對有利影響的領域在2025年底已到期的<平價醫療法案(ACA)>增強型保費補貼，受淨保
費上升，已有超過200萬人放棄投保，及受<大而美法案>被削減的醫療補助(Medicaid)在2025~2034年超過
9,000億美元。現ACA進行增強型補貼的恢復提案還在進行中，需總統簽署才能立法恢復，可以是談判交換條件
的一部分；Medicaid刪減是已成法、分階段生效，無法被推翻，只能延緩Medicaid的刪減，其中對工作的要求
限制於今年底生效，早於新國會就任，12/11臨時撥款法案談判是可以納入談判作為局部修正空間的時間點。
• 健護是民主黨國會最直接的受惠產業，健護未來一年本益比仍相對合理：預期若醫療補助刪減延後，醫院將受
惠，ACA補貼若部分恢復，將有利ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人數止跌回升。以健護未來一年本益比來看，

目前股價仍相對合理。

資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢
期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢
市場觀點
資料來源：(左) 高盛，2026/11；(右) 摩根大通，2026/11 。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11
分裂國會在兩年任期內的表現優於單一政黨執政
8
1974年以來標普500指數期中選舉前後的走勢
• 期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢：根據歷史經驗，期中選舉年通常是總統任期中回撤較深的一年，且選
前的股市波動會明顯放大，然在選後不確定性消退，市場表現往往有所改善，標普500指數在選後的3個月，歷
史中位數報酬率為+6%，而分裂政府(總統和國會分屬不同黨派)的歷史報酬率通常較佳。
• AI相關類股波動高，資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢：真正驅動市場的因素仍是利率、地緣、
AI，而不是單純從歷史規律推演，近期市場擔憂雲端業者的現金流和債務問題，因此若資料中心的建置時程受
到影響，將進一步影響相關業者的營收和償債時程，提高AI相關類股的波動；另外眾議院是否對川普提出彈劾，

也將造成政治上的波動。但就整體趨勢來說，既有政策不會因此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重要
支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢。
1950 年以來，不同政治格局下的標普500指數兩年價格報酬率

### Page 9

©  CTBC
短線待消化北亞出口疑慮，長線行情回歸基本面
情緒回穩後聚焦基本面與地緣動盪 短線消化資料中心建設監管趨嚴對北亞出口影響，
然長線美核心政策延續性依舊，估計影響有限
9
• 選後反彈高度取決景氣與政策推演：回顧過去美股期中選舉期間，新興市場選前壓抑，選後大多繳出正報酬佳
績，上漲比率超越五成。以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快速升息，
加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，選後新興市場進入近一年空頭走勢。2006年即便也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境受惠
貿易繁榮、商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個月累積報酬亮眼。總體來說，選後不確定性下降，
新興市場可望迎反彈走勢，而即便美國政治版圖翻轉，選後新興市場走勢取決於景氣、政策推演與地緣干擾。
• 市場短線待消化資料中心建設進程對北亞出口影響：美期中選舉對新興市場影響較大面向，主要是關稅與貿易
政策、債務上限，以及對資料中心建設的態度。關稅主導權在白宮推估影響有限 ; 預算與債限談判或對利率造
成波動 ; 短線需消化選後對資料中心監管趨嚴影響北亞出口的失望情緒，然預計長線AI建置仍屬國家重心政策。

資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/11，右， Bloomberg ，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11。

• 期中選舉共和黨大敗，美元走弱：週三市場確認共和黨於期中選舉失利後，美元兌主要貨幣全面回檔。本次期
中選舉由民主黨取得參、眾兩院多數。民主黨掌控參議院後，可阻止或延後川普的人事任命；贏得眾議院後，
則可對政府展開調查，甚至進一步推動彈劾程序。歷史經驗顯示，當民主黨於期中選舉後同時掌控參、眾兩院
時，美元指數平均下跌4.6%，6次中有4次下跌。整體而言，選舉結果短期內可能因預算協商及債務上限僵局

升高政治風險。2027年美國將再度面臨債務上限問題，美伊地緣政治及貿易戰等變數亦將牽動實質利率走勢。
• 2027年債務上限恐成兩黨鬥爭焦點，不利美元：12月11日前，現任國會通過持續撥款決議的可能性偏高，但
美國聯邦政府收支預計將於2027年觸及債務上限。受債務上限約束的債務規模預估於2027年3月接近法定上限
500億美元以內，之後隨4月稅收入帳而暫時下降；但若財政赤字增幅高於預期，X-Day恐提前到來。展望
2027年，美國財政議題仍將面臨逆風。共和黨失去完全執政雖有助抑制財政擴張，但政治僵局恐加劇債務上限、
違約及信評遭調降風險，使美元波動加大。

## Document 511: 中国：綱紀粛正強化と経済の萎縮

### Page 1

中国

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026 年 10 月 30 日 全 3 頁

中国：綱紀粛正強化と経済の萎縮 
中央委員は5 年で 2 割減の異常。問われることのない任命責任

調査本部 主席研究員 齋藤 尚登

[要約] 
 中国共産党第 20 期中央委員会第 5 回全体会議（五中全会）が、2026 年 10 月 26 日から
29 日にかけて北京で開催され、「中国共産党が全面的かつ厳格な統治を持続的に推進
するためのいくつかの重大問題に関する決定」を採択した。中国では汚職・腐敗撲滅、
綱紀粛正の嵐が吹いている。 
 今後、体制の引き締めが維持・強化される見通しであり、経済活動が一部萎縮するリ
スクに注意する必要がある。汚職・腐敗撲滅や綱紀粛正が強化される中、習近平総書
記への忠誠を高めることが最も重要なポイントとなる。この点で、習近平氏の関心が
どこに向かうかが決定的に重要だが、関心は経済よりも政治に向かいやすい。「景気テ
コ入れ」と「綱紀粛正」の指示が同時に出れば、「綱紀粛正」の影響が勝ることになろ
う。こうした状況下では、悪目立ちをしないでやり過ごす、「不作為」という状況に陥
りやすくなる。大和総研では、2027 年の成長率見通しを前年比 4.4％と、2026 年の同
4.6％から若干減速するとみているのは、この影響を懸念しているからだ。

### Page 2

1

2 / 3

汚職・腐敗撲滅、綱紀粛正の嵐 
中国共産党第 20 期中央委員会第 5 回全体会議（五中全会）が、2026 年 10 月 26 日から 29 日
にかけて北京で開催され、「中国共産党が全面的かつ厳格な統治を持続的に推進するためのい
くつかの重大問題に関する決定」（以下、決定）を採択して閉幕した。決定の中で印象的だっ
たのは「不敢腐、不能腐、不想腐」（腐敗させず、腐敗できず、腐敗したいと思わない）とい
うフレーズだ。腐敗・汚職撲滅と綱紀粛正の継続・強化が随所で言及されている。 
中国では汚職・腐敗撲滅、綱紀粛正の嵐が吹いている。五中全会に参加した中央委員は 162
名と発表された。2022 年 10 月に習近平政権 3 期目がスタートした当時は 205 名であり、それ
との比較では 21.0％の減少だ。ただし、この間に中央委員会候補委員から中央委員に昇格した
人々1がいることを考えると、減少の延べ人数はもっと多いことになる。全体の 2 割だとしても
異常な多さであり、汚職・腐敗撲滅、綱紀粛正の凄まじさを物語っている。こうした状況下で
習近平氏の任命責任が厳しく問われてもおかしくないが、そのような声は全く聞かれない。 
今回の五中全会では、馬興瑞・元政治局委員、張又侠・元政治局委員（元党中央軍事委員会
副主席）ら 33 名の党籍剥奪処分を改めて確認した。うち 16 名が軍部であった。党中央軍事委
員会は習近平政権の 3 期目発足時点では 7 名体制でスタートしたが、失脚に次ぐ失脚で、2026
年 1 月以降は習近平主席と張昇民副主席の 2 名体制となっていた（図表 1）。五中全会では、張
昇民副主席の政治局委員への昇格が発表されたが、党軍事委員会の補充は行われなかった。指
揮系統の中枢で 5 名分の空席が補充されない異常事態が続いている。

図表1 党中央軍事委員会メンバー（2022 年 10 月の発足当時からの変化）

「不作為」による経済の萎縮リスク 
五中全会では、中国共産党第 21 回全国代表大会を 2027 年下半期に北京で開催することが発
表された（通常は、10 月か 11 月に開催される）。これは 5 年に一度開催される最重要会議であ
り、直後の第 21 期中央委員会第 1 回全体会議（一中全会）で今後 5 年間の新指導体制が決定さ
れる。現状では、習近平総書記の 4 期目突入がほぼ確実視され、その後継候補となり得る 1970

### Page 3

1

3 / 3

年代生まれの人物が、中枢である中央政治局常務委員に抜擢されるかが焦点になっている。 
経済との関係でいえば、党大会が開催される西暦下一桁が 2 か 7 の年は、前年よりも成長率
が加速することが多かった（図表 2）。秋に党大会が開催され、翌年 3 月の全国人民代表大会
（全人代）では、国家機構の人事が決定される。中国共産党・政府関係者にとって、5 年に一度
の昇進の大チャンスが訪れることになる。自分が担当する地域や分野のパフォーマンスを向上
させることが出世の早道と目されたのである。 
しかし、今後、体制の引き締めが維持・強化される見通しであり、経済活動が一部萎縮する
リスクに注意する必要がある。汚職・腐敗撲滅や綱紀粛正が強化される中、習近平総書記への
忠誠を高めることが最も重要なポイントとなる。この点で、習近平氏の関心がどこに向かうか
が決定的に重要だ。結論を先にいえば、関心は経済よりも政治に向かいやすい。「景気テコ入
れ」と「綱紀粛正」の指示が同時に出れば、「綱紀粛正」の影響が勝ることになろう。こうし
た状況下では、悪目立ちをしないでやり過ごす、「不作為」という状況に陥りやすくなる。大
和総研では、2027 年の成長率見通しを前年比 4.4％と、2026 年の同 4.6％から若干減速すると
みているのは、この影響を懸念しているからだ。

## Document 512: 中期経済見通し（2026～2036年度）

Source PDF: all report pdf/中期経済見通し（2026～2036年度）.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 27
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

（備考）2025年度までの実績は内閣府「国民経済計算」により作成。

１. 世界の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は、2022 年以降３％台半ばで推移しているが、少子高齢化を背
景とした新興国の成長鈍化により、予測期間の終期の 2036 年には２％台後半まで低下す
ると予想する。 
２. 日本の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は、2036 年度までの先行き 10 年間の平均で 0.9％と予想する。
潜在成長率は足下のゼロ％台後半から 2020 年代後半に１％程度まで高まった後、少子高
齢化の更なる進展に伴い 2030年代前半にゼロ％台後半まで鈍化すると見込む。 
３. 消費者物価上昇率（生鮮食品を除く総合）は、先行き 10 年間の平均で 2.1％と予想する。
企業等の各経済主体の期待物価上昇率は２％前後となってきている。中東情勢の悪化に
よる物価上昇が落ち着くにつれ、2028 年度以降は、飲食料品の消費税率の引下げ・引上
げの影響を除けば２％程度の安定的な物価上昇が実現すると見込む。 
４. 日本銀行は、当面は物価上昇が再燃する中、消費者物価上昇率を２％程度に安定させてい
くため、段階的に利上げを実施し、政策金利は 2027 年度に1.75％まで引き上げられると
見込む。長期金利は、長期国債の買い入れ減額の継続に加え、国内の貯蓄超過の縮小が上
昇圧力になることから、2036 年度には３％台後半まで上昇すると予想する。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-13

中期経済見通し（2026～2036 年度）

経済研究部 
(03)3512-1884

### Page 2

主要先進国の成長率はコロナ禍で大きく落ち込んだものの、その後は相次ぐショックにもかかわ
らず底堅く推移している。ホルムズ海峡の輸送が制約される中では、世界的な原油在庫の取り崩し、
エネルギー需要抑制や政府主導での節約措置、供給経路・供給源の多様化（中東産原油の代替ルー
トでの輸送や中東以外の原油増産）などが供給制約ショックを吸収している。 
インフレ率は 2022 年のエネルギー価格高騰などを背景に急上昇したのち、2025 年にかけて緩や
かに低下してきた。しかし、コロナ禍前の水準まで低下しないまま、中東情勢の悪化で再び上昇圧
力が強まっている。2022年のインフレ加速期に、主要中央銀行による金融引き締めが遅れた経験も
あり、中央銀行はインフレ警戒感を強めており、利上げに転じる国も増えつつある。

（ＡＩ関連需要が成長率を押し上げ） 
成長率の底堅さの要因として、足もとで顕著となっているのが世界的なＡＩ関連需要の高まりで
ある。半導体など電子部品需要、データセンターを中心とした建設需要、電力などのインフラ投資
需要が増加している。世界半導体売上高（3か月移動平均）は急増しており、2026 年5 月以降、前
年比で 2倍を超えている。米中を中心にＡＩ開発が急速に進んでいるほか、ＡＩ関連需要は貿易を
通じて特にアジアの生産・輸出を押し上げるなど、世界に波及している。

### Page 3

こうした状況下、主要先進国では長期金利の上昇圧力が強まっている。インフレ圧力の強まりと
中央銀行の利上げ観測のほか、政府の財政支出拡大懸念や民間部門での資金調達需要が金利の押し
上げ圧力となっている。 
財政支出に関して、米国では主要財源と見られた関税引き上げ政策のうち、最高裁判所により国
際緊急経済権限法（ＩＥＥＰＡ）に基づく関税が無効と判断され、歳入の不透明感が増した。欧州
ではトランプ政権による安全保障政策の方針転換を背景に防衛費を拡大する動きが進む。日本では、
ガソリン価格抑制策など物価高対策が長期化するなか、「責任ある積極財政」を掲げる高市政権の
財政支出に注目が集まっている。民間部門の資金調達需要の高まりは、巨大ＩＴ企業によるＡＩ関
連投資の拡大などが背景にある。

（経済を取り巻く不確実性は引き続き高い） 
世界経済を取り巻く不確実性は高い状態が続いている。トランプ大統領の講じる政策は予見可能
性が低い。今後も通商領域やその他の領域で国家間の摩擦が強まる可能性がある。短期的な経済シ
ョックが生じうるだけでなく、中長期的にはトランプ大統領の米国第一主義や既存の国際秩序や多
国間枠組みを見直す政策姿勢がドル資産への需要やドルの基軸通貨としての地位に影響を及ぼす
可能性もあるだろう。地政学的リスクも大きく、すでにウクライナや中東地域では地政学的要因が
経済に悪影響を及ぼしている。原油在庫の取り崩しや供給源の多様化といったバッファーには量的
